You can view Michelle's version of the workshop on Github </span><a class="XqQF9c" href="https://github.com/MicheleTobias/Workshop-Cartography-Journal-Figures" target="_blank" style="color: inherit; text-decoration: none;"><span class="C9DxTc aw5Odc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700; text-decoration: underline;">here</span></a><span class="C9DxTc">.</span></small></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.12cadfe7927db3eb_0" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.12cadfe7927db3eb_3" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">This workshop will cover best practices for creating map figures for journals articles and academic texts. The exercises are designed to be run independently using </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">QGIS 3.14</span><span class="C9DxTc">. By the end of the workshop students will have gained the skills and information needed to create maps that meet the unique graphics requirements of typical journal publication while still being effective visual communication tools.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.704e3d85fd0fb8e6_7" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-uQSCkd Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-uQSCkd purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.704e3d85fd0fb8e6_4" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-uQSCkd Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-uQSCkd jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><a class="XqQF9c" href="https://sites.google.com/stanford.edu/gis-cartography/workshops/maps-for-academic-journals#h.3iex1262u59w" target="_blank" style="color: inherit; margin-left: 0; margin-right: 0; padding: 0;"><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Desktop GIS software, and QGIS specifically. Please see our </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Intro to QGIS</span><span class="C9DxTc"> workshop page first if you are new to GIS software or need a refresher.</span></p></li></ul></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.10f935aaed86c693_32" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.10f935aaed86c693_35" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><div id="h.aqhrst9xy6ex" class="GV3q8e yMxPgf aP9Z7e"></div><h3 id="h.aqhrst9xy6ex_l" dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q OmQG5e CDt4Ke"><div jscontroller="Ae65rd" jsaction="touchstart:UrsOsc; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Download and install </span><a class="XqQF9c" href="https://qgis.org/en/site/forusers/download.html" target="_blank" style="color: inherit; text-decoration: none;"><span class="C9DxTc aw5Odc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700; text-decoration: underline;">QGIS</span></a><span class="C9DxTc"> - a free and open source desktop GIS for Mac, Windows, and Linux.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; text-decoration: none;"><span class="C9DxTc aw5Odc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700; text-decoration: underline;">data collection</span></a><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;"> </span><span class="C9DxTc">and</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;"> </span><span class="C9DxTc">extract it to your Desktop.</span></p></li></ul></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.10f935aaed86c693_36" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.10f935aaed86c693_39" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><div id="h.t75s002fm76y" class="GV3q8e yMxPgf aP9Z7e"></div><h3 id="h.t75s002fm76y_l" dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q OmQG5e CDt4Ke"><div jscontroller="Ae65rd" jsaction="touchstart:UrsOsc; click:KjsqPd; focusout:QZoaZ; mouseover:y0pDld; mouseout:dq0hvd;fv1Rjc:jbFSOd;CrfLRd:SzACGe;" class="CjVfdc"><div class="PPhIP rviiZ" jsname="haAclf"><div role="presentation" class="U26fgb mUbCce fKz7Od LRAOtb Znu9nd M9Bg4d" jscontroller="mxS5xe" jsaction="click:cOuCgd; mousedown:UX7yZ; mouseup:lbsD7e; mouseenter:tfO1Yc; mouseleave:JywGue; focus:AHmuwe; blur:O22p3e; contextmenu:mg9Pef;" jsshadow="" aria-describedby="h.t75s002fm76y_l" aria-label="Copy heading link" aria-disabled="false" data-tooltip="Copy heading link" aria-hidden="true" data-tooltip-position="top" data-tooltip-vertical-offset="12" data-tooltip-horizontal-offset="0"><a class="FKF6mc TpQm9d" href="#h.t75s002fm76y" aria-label="Copy heading link" jsname="hiK3ld" role="button" aria-describedby="h.t75s002fm76y_l"><div class="VTBa7b MbhUzd" jsname="ksKsZd"></div><span jsslot="" class="xjKiLb"><span class="Ce1Y1c" style="top: -11px"><svg class="OUGEr QdAdhf" width="22px" height="22px" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" /></span></span></a></div></div><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Background</span></div></h3><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Maps, graphs and other figures are the unsung heroes of academic publication. The content may be in the text, but maps and other illustrations are often where our eyes stop as we flip and scan through articles. They represent opportunities for quick bursts of densely packed visual communication.&#160;</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Academic publication presents a few unique challenges to creating effective maps and learning to work around those constraints requires a different approach than we might use for larger maps.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">The most common design issues for journal publication are:</span></p><ul class="n8H08c UVNKR" style="list-style-type: square; margin-left: 0; margin-right: 0; padding: 0;"><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">small map sizes</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">limited color (or grayscale only)</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">low resolution</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">data citation and licensing</span></p></li></ul></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.662cbafc8a48a809_0" class="yaqOZd lQAHbd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.662cbafc8a48a809_3" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><div id="h.7s77m3z85iyl" class="CobnVe yMxPgf aP9Z7e"></div><h2 id="h.7s77m3z85iyl_l" dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q JYVBee CDt4Ke"><div jscontroller="Ae65rd" jsaction="touchstart:UrsOsc; click:KjsqPd; focusout:QZoaZ; mouseover:y0pDld; mouseout:dq0hvd;fv1Rjc:jbFSOd;CrfLRd:SzACGe;" class="CjVfdc"><div class="PPhIP rviiZ" jsname="haAclf"><div role="presentation" class="U26fgb mUbCce fKz7Od LRAOtb Znu9nd M9Bg4d" jscontroller="mxS5xe" jsaction="click:cOuCgd; mousedown:UX7yZ; mouseup:lbsD7e; mouseenter:tfO1Yc; mouseleave:JywGue; focus:AHmuwe; blur:O22p3e; contextmenu:mg9Pef;" jsshadow="" aria-describedby="h.7s77m3z85iyl_l" aria-label="Copy heading link" aria-disabled="false" data-tooltip="Copy heading link" aria-hidden="true" data-tooltip-position="top" data-tooltip-vertical-offset="12" data-tooltip-horizontal-offset="0"><a class="FKF6mc TpQm9d" href="#h.7s77m3z85iyl" aria-label="Copy heading link" jsname="hiK3ld" role="button" aria-describedby="h.7s77m3z85iyl_l"><div class="VTBa7b MbhUzd" jsname="ksKsZd"></div><span jsslot="" class="xjKiLb"><span class="Ce1Y1c" style="top: -11px"><svg class="OUGEr QdAdhf" width="22px" height="22px" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" /></span></span></a></div></div><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Steps for creating journal map figures</span></div></h2></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_3" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_0" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><div id="h.603rf7c63s44" class="GV3q8e yMxPgf aP9Z7e"></div><h3 id="h.603rf7c63s44_l" dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q OmQG5e CDt4Ke"><div jscontroller="Ae65rd" jsaction="touchstart:UrsOsc; click:KjsqPd; focusout:QZoaZ; mouseover:y0pDld; mouseout:dq0hvd;fv1Rjc:jbFSOd;CrfLRd:SzACGe;" class="CjVfdc"><div class="PPhIP rviiZ" jsname="haAclf"><div role="presentation" class="U26fgb mUbCce fKz7Od LRAOtb Znu9nd M9Bg4d" jscontroller="mxS5xe" jsaction="click:cOuCgd; mousedown:UX7yZ; mouseup:lbsD7e; mouseenter:tfO1Yc; mouseleave:JywGue; focus:AHmuwe; blur:O22p3e; contextmenu:mg9Pef;" jsshadow="" aria-describedby="h.603rf7c63s44_l" aria-label="Copy heading link" aria-disabled="false" data-tooltip="Copy heading link" aria-hidden="true" data-tooltip-position="top" data-tooltip-vertical-offset="12" data-tooltip-horizontal-offset="0"><a class="FKF6mc TpQm9d" href="#h.603rf7c63s44" aria-label="Copy heading link" jsname="hiK3ld" role="button" aria-describedby="h.603rf7c63s44_l"><div class="VTBa7b MbhUzd" jsname="ksKsZd"></div><span jsslot="" class="xjKiLb"><span class="Ce1Y1c" style="top: -11px"><svg class="OUGEr QdAdhf" width="22px" height="22px" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" /></span></span></a></div></div><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">What map are you making?</span></div></h3><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">The first step in making any map is understanding the message you are trying to convey, along with identifying who you are talking to. Your map should be designed to address the specific topic and the audience who will see it. Here are a couple of real world examples:</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">The map on the left was done for full color book publication, accompanying the memoirs of Secretary John Kerry and depicts his travels by "swift boat" during the Vietnam war. The use of color and detail here draw attention to the boats path, but keeps enough context for readers likely unfamiliar with the regions geography.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">The map on the right was a journal figure on the loss of habitat and range of China's Giant Pandas. Print limitations meant no color and a small design space. But readers were assumed to be familiar with the subject so the choice was made to leave background details off and focus instead on the the core provinces and panda habitat.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_11" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_8" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0STAvISo9CwgoR1ouAuzo1ACse_jiTUhksUvOg3RxOfLI7hIwZSgL3Qd0JnlrCCEJw-zKW6jzTJl3tL6bY4NLAhFHZnHs3M7B5-KhGOskU1Bq85cjGrr8uXZKUOlhWMsKtfeqHJ5CwyuZ59Q_wXpJouR4fsFUm5E5uo=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_12" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_164" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><small dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TMjjoe CDt4Ke" style="display: block;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-style: italic;">Swift Boat Route and Great Panda Habitat maps, David Medeiros.</span></small></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_19" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc yYI8W HQwdzb"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_18" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><div id="h.6mzl749k4pt" class="GV3q8e yMxPgf aP9Z7e"></div><h3 id="h.6mzl749k4pt_l" dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q OmQG5e CDt4Ke"><div jscontroller="Ae65rd" jsaction="touchstart:UrsOsc; click:KjsqPd; focusout:QZoaZ; mouseover:y0pDld; mouseout:dq0hvd;fv1Rjc:jbFSOd;CrfLRd:SzACGe;" class="CjVfdc"><div class="PPhIP rviiZ" jsname="haAclf"><div role="presentation" class="U26fgb mUbCce fKz7Od LRAOtb Znu9nd M9Bg4d" jscontroller="mxS5xe" jsaction="click:cOuCgd; mousedown:UX7yZ; mouseup:lbsD7e; mouseenter:tfO1Yc; mouseleave:JywGue; focus:AHmuwe; blur:O22p3e; contextmenu:mg9Pef;" jsshadow="" aria-describedby="h.6mzl749k4pt_l" aria-label="Copy heading link" aria-disabled="false" data-tooltip="Copy heading link" aria-hidden="true" data-tooltip-position="top" data-tooltip-vertical-offset="12" data-tooltip-horizontal-offset="0"><a class="FKF6mc TpQm9d" href="#h.6mzl749k4pt" aria-label="Copy heading link" jsname="hiK3ld" role="button" aria-describedby="h.6mzl749k4pt_l"><div class="VTBa7b MbhUzd" jsname="ksKsZd"></div><span jsslot="" class="xjKiLb"><span class="Ce1Y1c" style="top: -11px"><svg class="OUGEr QdAdhf" width="22px" height="22px" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" /></span></span></a></div></div><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Data</span></div></h3><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">You will need geospatial data to build your map with. You may already have some data on hand from any related GIS work you've done. But be wary of using analysis data for map figures. Raw GIS data is often too dense or detailed for small figures. What looks ok on a desktop will quickly become an illegible mess when printed at 3" wide on medium weight paper.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Be conscious of matching your data scale to your map scale, which simply means don't use overly detailed or overly simplified data for the map size being produced. Visit </span><a class="XqQF9c" href="https://www.naturalearthdata.com/downloads/" target="_blank" style="color: inherit; text-decoration: none;"><span class="C9DxTc aw5Odc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700; text-decoration: underline;">Natural Earth Data</span></a><span class="C9DxTc"> for a selection of general purpose GIS data at three different scales.</span></p></div></div></div></div><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_34" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SQ_i5Ocq97CYdTqGVQEYp9iIZfW-PP7resj4EygqJNG4rj6sRAc3Y1HCaVpkzeCGZJ48ifhOvR-ipoMx6KtEyJz_ayf3Sa5H373_eUzjiwLiR6zHJzxVcj1ZEbhXKFTovCWT7ICHGqk9rAADgvVkfUipBq7cD3jhDgKQfiU-13wkEm7g2-Acza3wnHiT1UTJieyfEhY1p_-AG4=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_20" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_23" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><div id="h.fr9sftfjizkn" class="GV3q8e yMxPgf aP9Z7e"></div><h3 id="h.fr9sftfjizkn_l" dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q OmQG5e CDt4Ke"><div jscontroller="Ae65rd" jsaction="touchstart:UrsOsc; click:KjsqPd; focusout:QZoaZ; mouseover:y0pDld; mouseout:dq0hvd;fv1Rjc:jbFSOd;CrfLRd:SzACGe;" class="CjVfdc"><div class="PPhIP rviiZ" jsname="haAclf"><div role="presentation" class="U26fgb mUbCce fKz7Od LRAOtb Znu9nd M9Bg4d" jscontroller="mxS5xe" jsaction="click:cOuCgd; mousedown:UX7yZ; mouseup:lbsD7e; mouseenter:tfO1Yc; mouseleave:JywGue; focus:AHmuwe; blur:O22p3e; contextmenu:mg9Pef;" jsshadow="" aria-describedby="h.fr9sftfjizkn_l" aria-label="Copy heading link" aria-disabled="false" data-tooltip="Copy heading link" aria-hidden="true" data-tooltip-position="top" data-tooltip-vertical-offset="12" data-tooltip-horizontal-offset="0"><a class="FKF6mc TpQm9d" href="#h.fr9sftfjizkn" aria-label="Copy heading link" jsname="hiK3ld" role="button" aria-describedby="h.fr9sftfjizkn_l"><div class="VTBa7b MbhUzd" jsname="ksKsZd"></div><span jsslot="" class="xjKiLb"><span class="Ce1Y1c" style="top: -11px"><svg class="OUGEr QdAdhf" width="22px" height="22px" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" /></span></span></a></div></div><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Journal art specifications</span></div></h3><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">We still haven't made a map yet, but I promise we're getting there! There's one more set of details we need before we start actually creating a map, the journals art specifications. Every journal (book, and magazine) will have a set of specs that guide submission for images and illustrations. Because we're generally talking about print publication, the restrictions can be quite limiting in terms of what can actually be submitted. If you fail to meet the art guidelines for a given publisher you risk not having your maps included with your writing, or possibly worse, having the publisher modify your maps for you!</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Let's look at one real-world example: </span><a class="XqQF9c" href="https://www.nature.com/nature/for-authors/formatting-guide" target="_blank" style="color: inherit; text-decoration: none;"><span class="C9DxTc aw5Odc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700; text-decoration: underline;">Nature's Journals</span></a></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">I look for the following pieces of information:</span></p><ul class="n8H08c UVNKR" style="list-style-type: square; margin-left: 0; margin-right: 0; padding: 0;"><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Maximum figure size</span><span class="C9DxTc">: 89 mm (spans 1 column) or 183 mm (spans 2 columns) wide and maximum 247 mm high. This means our figures should be one of the two width dimensions and not a variation. We can have whatever height we need up to the maximum.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Aspect ratio restrictions</span><span class="C9DxTc">: none listed. That's fine because we have very specific dimensions.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Color guidance or restrictions</span><span class="C9DxTc">: Nature's Journal uses four-color reproduction (they use cyan, magenta, yellow, and black (CMYK) inks for printed material), but they charge for color figures. If they charge for color, try to make a grayscale map.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Font guidance or restrictions</span><span class="C9DxTc">: none listed for maps. Some journals will specify a particular font or that the font must be an open font. I suggest sticking with something fairly common unless you really need a special font.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Format and quality</span><span class="C9DxTc">: electronic format, suggesting .jpg files "at good enough quality to be assessed by referees". You'll eventually submit higher quality figures for publication but not for the review process. Some journals specify things like .eps files or .tiff at a certain dpi and may also expect a certain color space (like CMYK, RGB). Maps and other graphic figures are usually ok at 300 dpi given the small print sizes. Larger maps, full color maps, and maps with a lot of detail may benefit from being saved at 600 dpi. Note that if you can't supply an .eps file, a plain .svg is usually readable by Adobe Illustrator with minimal issues.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Other limitations</span><span class="C9DxTc">: Nature does not want figures to have separate panels within the same figure unless they are related to each other. This means we can only have one map per figure unless they are related.</span></p></li></ul><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Publishers vary in how much detail they give up front about these specifications and in may cases you will need to piece together specs for maps from different sections of the author instructions. Parts of the figure specs, photography, or graphs may apply. Be persistent, there is no point in starting a map without these details.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_36" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_39" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><div id="h.whbdgqpt3bi3" class="GV3q8e yMxPgf aP9Z7e"></div><h3 id="h.whbdgqpt3bi3_l" dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q OmQG5e CDt4Ke"><div jscontroller="Ae65rd" jsaction="touchstart:UrsOsc; click:KjsqPd; focusout:QZoaZ; mouseover:y0pDld; mouseout:dq0hvd;fv1Rjc:jbFSOd;CrfLRd:SzACGe;" class="CjVfdc"><div class="PPhIP rviiZ" jsname="haAclf"><div role="presentation" class="U26fgb mUbCce fKz7Od LRAOtb Znu9nd M9Bg4d" jscontroller="mxS5xe" jsaction="click:cOuCgd; mousedown:UX7yZ; mouseup:lbsD7e; mouseenter:tfO1Yc; mouseleave:JywGue; focus:AHmuwe; blur:O22p3e; contextmenu:mg9Pef;" jsshadow="" aria-describedby="h.whbdgqpt3bi3_l" aria-label="Copy heading link" aria-disabled="false" data-tooltip="Copy heading link" aria-hidden="true" data-tooltip-position="top" data-tooltip-vertical-offset="12" data-tooltip-horizontal-offset="0"><a class="FKF6mc TpQm9d" href="#h.whbdgqpt3bi3" aria-label="Copy heading link" jsname="hiK3ld" role="button" aria-describedby="h.whbdgqpt3bi3_l"><div class="VTBa7b MbhUzd" jsname="ksKsZd"></div><span jsslot="" class="xjKiLb"><span class="Ce1Y1c" style="top: -11px"><svg class="OUGEr QdAdhf" width="22px" height="22px" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" /></span></span></a></div></div><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Page setup</span></div></h3><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">After starting up the GIS and adding your initial data layers, the very next thing you should do is go into the print composer/layout view and set your output page dimensions. Refer to your art specifications for the exact sizes required. It's best to set the page size to the max width dimensions and adjust the length dimensions to suit the map layout. You might want to use a standard aspect ratio like </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">1:1 </span><span class="C9DxTc">&#160;</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">4:3</span><span class="C9DxTc"> or </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">3:2</span><span class="C9DxTc"> to maximize your maps compatibility with other display options. So if your max width is 90mm at 3:2, your max height would be 135mm (or about 3.5"x5.3").</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Don't leave this step for the end as you'll end up building a map to the default page size of 8.5"x11" and be forced to shrink this to fit the journal specs later, or rebuild the map entirely.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_40" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_43" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><div id="h.72avo0dwvzbz" class="GV3q8e yMxPgf aP9Z7e"></div><h3 id="h.72avo0dwvzbz_l" dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q OmQG5e CDt4Ke"><div jscontroller="Ae65rd" jsaction="touchstart:UrsOsc; click:KjsqPd; focusout:QZoaZ; mouseover:y0pDld; mouseout:dq0hvd;fv1Rjc:jbFSOd;CrfLRd:SzACGe;" class="CjVfdc"><div class="PPhIP rviiZ" jsname="haAclf"><div role="presentation" class="U26fgb mUbCce fKz7Od LRAOtb Znu9nd M9Bg4d" jscontroller="mxS5xe" jsaction="click:cOuCgd; mousedown:UX7yZ; mouseup:lbsD7e; mouseenter:tfO1Yc; mouseleave:JywGue; focus:AHmuwe; blur:O22p3e; contextmenu:mg9Pef;" jsshadow="" aria-describedby="h.72avo0dwvzbz_l" aria-label="Copy heading link" aria-disabled="false" data-tooltip="Copy heading link" aria-hidden="true" data-tooltip-position="top" data-tooltip-vertical-offset="12" data-tooltip-horizontal-offset="0"><a class="FKF6mc TpQm9d" href="#h.72avo0dwvzbz" aria-label="Copy heading link" jsname="hiK3ld" role="button" aria-describedby="h.72avo0dwvzbz_l"><div class="VTBa7b MbhUzd" jsname="ksKsZd"></div><span jsslot="" class="xjKiLb"><span class="Ce1Y1c" style="top: -11px"><svg class="OUGEr QdAdhf" width="22px" height="22px" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" /></span></span></a></div></div><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Design</span></div></h3><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">The goal in map design overall is to create something that is clear, legible, and communicates the intended message (and nothing else!).</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Here are a few general concepts to keep in mind when making your map figures.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Visual Hierarchy&#160;</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Visual hierarchy is the separation of images into background and foreground elements (aka </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">figure</span><span class="C9DxTc"> and </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">ground</span><span class="C9DxTc">). The order of our maps features in the visual hierarchy suggests their relative importance to the maps message. Ground elements are contextual while figure elements represent our data. Anything that stands out from the background will rise in the visual order. More important features should move to the front with less important contextual map elements moving to the back. Similar features should reside near each other in the visual hierarchy while dissimilar features should be separated. Maps with a flat visual hierarchy can be difficult to interpret.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_82" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_79" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SRCDAz3zcIznG0aUBmYh9RIdMg2Br03c2TcHiWr5-qEgzcCaAge7UzpYGbIaHZtQsDUVUR1NY7lUh9mzSBmq3hiEoXbkaYutaGWYZGINI6Dkh-JA3wYG3jHKd8BW3l5J3b-1DO3oDhSjxnlIB4BQPJF7SxYvFHljnru-wc3BrIV1olB6YogXGVvaJ7V7J9b7K4z08cmf6b3OLg=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_75" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div jscontroller="rVn3Q" jsaction="rcuQ6b:rcuQ6b; keydown:bNMLAd; click:KjsqPd;MxH79b:Fvx5s; touchstart:UrsOsc; touchend:V7HmAd; focus:G83JOb; blur:gX3lbf;AHmuwe:G83JOb;O22p3e:gX3lbf;" class="mr3rhf" style="aspect-ratio: 0.7877094972067039 / 1;" tabindex="0" data-autostart="true" data-showdots="true" data-showcaptions="false" data-transitionspeed="1800" aria-label="Image carousel"><div jsname="kEMzy" class="Ah1ayc" aria-live="polite"><div style="background-image: url(https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SS7M-x0L5d_VncTCeVmIOpM1mYAUJVDqYoPsPUxz7aIrbgV3pRqqP92WC7sWZPmiDnIQTnRgTtLXIkb0DwdaoPBdC0fS3zgpa278bxkpu9wkWU2LS_OOyZl4JGj1ODYDykOACen-1_NDXcmDnk9U6qp7XhzEg_4=w16383);z-index: 1;" class="nQBJnb" role="img" jsname="hcY42b" aria-label="Carousel image"></div><div style="background-image: url(https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SQAc5agU-ilRGZL0lU4aHFzWxAn2j2f6eC1qwX1javwNJSjdsCQZpU0ycnUSVcoBGP4UN-3O-KzjCi1q6bMza4j8CYYYfCG-qxS3K09gtD97P1vS3oxTVJcSXFxaoBESsq3-ASStTgeYvK5FMhjFDL9SeH8uJ82=w16383);" class="nQBJnb" role="img" jsname="hcY42b" aria-label="Carousel image"></div><div jsaction="JIbuQc:LLzMhd;"><div role="button" class="uArJ5e Y5FYJe VsxsTb B43yq Eoo44b" jscontroller="VXdfxd" jsaction="click:cOuCgd; mousedown:UX7yZ; mouseup:lbsD7e; mouseenter:tfO1Yc; mouseleave:JywGue;touchstart:p6p2H; touchmove:FwuNnf; touchend:yfqBxc(preventDefault=true); touchcancel:JMtRjd;focus:AHmuwe; blur:O22p3e; contextmenu:mg9Pef;" jsshadow="" jsname="VfNHU" aria-label="Previous" tabindex="0" data-tooltip="Previous" data-tooltip-vertical-offset="-12" data-tooltip-horizontal-offset="0"><div class="PDXc1b MbhUzd" jsname="ksKsZd"></div><span jsslot="" class="XuQwKc"><span class="GmuOkf"><svg width="36px" height="36px" viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="currentColor" /></span></span></div></div><div jsaction="JIbuQc:KmWemb;"><div role="button" class="uArJ5e Y5FYJe VsxsTb eb4Tkf Eoo44b" jscontroller="VXdfxd" jsaction="click:cOuCgd; mousedown:UX7yZ; mouseup:lbsD7e; mouseenter:tfO1Yc; mouseleave:JywGue;touchstart:p6p2H; touchmove:FwuNnf; touchend:yfqBxc(preventDefault=true); touchcancel:JMtRjd;focus:AHmuwe; blur:O22p3e; contextmenu:mg9Pef;" jsshadow="" jsname="OCpkoe" aria-label="Next" tabindex="0" data-tooltip="Next" data-tooltip-vertical-offset="-12" data-tooltip-horizontal-offset="0"><div class="PDXc1b MbhUzd" jsname="ksKsZd"></div><span jsslot="" class="XuQwKc"><span class="GmuOkf"><svg width="36px" height="36px" viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="currentColor" /></span></span></div></div></div><div class="cc0d9d" jsname="Hj7utc"><span class="ZbQFKd CbiMKe" jsname="VeXVo"></span><span class="ZbQFKd" jsname="VeXVo"></span></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_156" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><small dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TMjjoe CDt4Ke" style="display: block;"><span class="C9DxTc">Lighter grays push some features to the back while dark gray and full black cause others to rise to the top of visual order. </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-size: 9.0pt; font-style: italic; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline;">Making Maps, Third Edition: A Visual Guide to Map Design for GIS</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-style: italic;">.</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-size: 9.0pt; font-style: italic; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline;"> </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-style: italic; text-decoration: none;">John Krygier, Deni</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-style: italic;">s Wood.</span></small><br></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_59" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_62" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">V</span><span class="C9DxTc">isual hierarchy can be affected by changing a number of different symbol variables like </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">size</span><span class="C9DxTc">, </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">color</span><span class="C9DxTc">, and </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">contrast</span><span class="C9DxTc">. Anything that helps separate features from their background will help establish them in the visual order.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_175" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-qWD73c purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_172" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-qWD73c jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SSacJWwGGMpseD1dWdFEY-btvYLqPF06ThRyR8cusGIL861b93FHV_HbZiupvXc6VTFlXnXwmAeQN3O_08CgN9kP_vkTcOw9CAsMAx8SGyBLOWfHyxpDSrClIrLVqx5eckFC2PE5ZLghFf_PvBfIzUrfoaFXo8iCxY=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img" style="width: 100%; margin: 0% 0 0% 0%"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_168" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_171" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Academic publications often limit us to black and white printing, but you can still create a deep visual hierarchy with no color by using different shades of gray to establish order.&#160; </span><span class="C9DxTc">Adjusting the amount of black in your map symbols will allow you to create a strong and varied hierarchy even when restricted to black and white printing. Anything between 10% and 90% will read as 'ground'. The greater the percent black difference the greater the visual contrast. Save full black for your data or 'figure'. Full white usually reads as empty or 'no data'.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_179" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-wNfPc purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ktthjf-AhqUyc-wNfPc JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_176" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-wNfPc pSzOP-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ktthjf-AhqUyc-wNfPc jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SQZdDUWlgc4QKxhOooF7zRNj9tS_yggb8TnAZwUWZgDwID_O7x7I54eropcM-XWajYuUJXtV5J52C_1T5PyFm49jLE1C_fZkrpYcKjMwiSFPG8gGVs7I04XhZfiidB7UJ4LiXawzDjfAS-pDi-z-VSbFelTcsVvcRQDMx4ECD22pyWrosT3yPeIaY3VFOpKUQ0lVxyhRtMj=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img" style="width: 100.0000026907%; margin: -0% 0 -0% -0.0000013454%"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-wNfPc purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_180" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-wNfPc pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><small dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TMjjoe CDt4Ke" style="display: block;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-style: italic;">This map of panda habitat uses different shades of gray to create a visual hierarchy. Lighter gray areas create the background. China is identified with a slightly darker gray. The historic panda range is noticeably darker then the background, helping identify it as more important to the maps message. Finally the current panda range is in full black lifting it to the top of the visual hierarchy and making sure it stands out against much larger areas.</span></small></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_87" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_90" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Fonts</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Font should be kept simple. Follow the art specifications but in the absence of specific recommendations, stick with well know common fonts. Avoid decorative fonts or using more than one or two fonts in a single map figure.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Use changes in the style of a single font family (</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">bold</span><span class="C9DxTc"> &#160; SIZE &#160; s p a c i n g &#160; </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-style: italic;">italics&#160; </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #6fa8dc;">color</span><span class="C9DxTc">) to create a variety of font styles that link them to specific feature types and reinforce the maps visual hierarchy.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_94" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-wNfPc purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ktthjf-AhqUyc-wNfPc JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_95" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-wNfPc pSzOP-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ktthjf-AhqUyc-wNfPc jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SQW3N5jasn6UEu_XGPRQamqg1VNNwEiE9rB3x1wVXl-oUM4u0OeRagY3O50DVNjvL4bHsEiWofzwnVYnb7p1FjweXqx4NJNbFqy5oSh6xBLu2DZCgcjAJq7C4hvS7C2dfvu4uXhCp-Z1t09h5K9ixg_OYEBMb03IBfU98q-neofKiZqQX-gsc_ZhrXGZVV5AQvE7GW6dwGR=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img" style="width: 100.0000030372%; margin: 0% 0 0% -0.0000015186%"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-wNfPc purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ktthjf-AhqUyc-wNfPc JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_91" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-wNfPc pSzOP-AhqUyc-wNfPc Ktthjf-AhqUyc-wNfPc jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SRXC-x8CG1NqFDRfc4MleK7PrCCOiUtJFYu4Di5M1pvJlqZe-Ulo3ddUTR9D_V5DM6zAaylcSbvccAyUWKyINUsDokjq-7EIOaPIPXG6NA8uyikV04aZ1-oQjptA4CygO0OnDMHbKEFTTtHtjJ64U6NZQhzQXbyIPPaRT11LtyKVAmyCCQkRGpWAlJXfRAvCfc__j7zcm65Ce4=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img" style="width: 100%; font-weight: 700;">Color</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">In most cases you'll be restricted to grayscale printing due to cost or other print limitations. When color is an option, you're best served by continuing to use grayscale for the background map elements and only adding color for your data. With a subdued map base, small amounts of saturated color will stand out clearly and create a dramatic effect in the visual hierarchy. Stick with natural colors over harsh bright colors and be mindful of natural mapping between colors and features (like reserving blue for water or cold).</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><small dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TMjjoe CDt4Ke" style="display: block;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-style: italic;">Autumn in Somerville, A. Woodruff, Bostonography 2011. Hurricane Risk, University of Miami.</span></small></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_112" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_115" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">When including data that has sequential values (such as a raster for sea surface temperatures, cold to warm), avoid the temptation to use the 'rainbow' color ramp. Although commonly used in scientific visualizations, there is a solid body of research that suggests the rainbow color scheme can actually lead to misinterpretation of the information being visualized. You can read more about the pitfalls and solutions to rainbow color ramps </span><a class="XqQF9c" href="https://earthobservatory.nasa.gov/blogs/elegantfigures/2013/08/05/subtleties-of-color-part-1-of-6/" target="_blank" style="color: inherit; text-decoration: none;"><span class="C9DxTc aw5Odc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700; text-decoration: underline;">here</span></a><span class="C9DxTc">, </span><a class="XqQF9c" href="https://eagereyes.org/basics/rainbow-color-map" target="_blank" style="color: inherit; text-decoration: none;"><span class="C9DxTc aw5Odc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700; text-decoration: underline;">here</span></a><span class="C9DxTc">, and </span><a class="XqQF9c" href="https://agilescientific.com/blog/2017/12/14/no-more-rainbows" target="_blank" style="color: inherit; text-decoration: none;"><span class="C9DxTc aw5Odc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700; text-decoration: underline;">here</span></a><span class="C9DxTc">!</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Instead map sequential values as a single color going from light to dark. Or if the data is divergent (breaking away from a central value) you can use a 2 color divergent scheme.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" baZpAe"><small dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TMjjoe CDt4Ke" style="display: block;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Rainbows are for unicorns!</span></small></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_102" class="yaqOZd qeLZfd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_95" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SRcTd4Qm2fUxKokc5E4DvjGYctVwRFhkrgnaqu3YqNBp0Q96WQFKi7t1roLNha0Z1LTQ22bJ-QuHKOozFJ0YuLL2CdSfMRZya2J39XUwJTLzcEGpT0kPCyNHUbTwazqqcpzjkKzYkd53bGrIEiyuHjwdhfp_ks2WVYFs2Zx197jwEzzJkm_pPZdxjZNQ3PM2uxSWShtG5wo-c4oWIQysk8rhWC1lXpE8uD5=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6dec52a75def9f7c_0" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SQ0OHl52AsOkfqXYZx_N0PEPI_kRF1O46_QlOA7ZVPCZ1cziFnxYy-9vWK4Ztca_lOad3odJ-BBge4UZTje0RFtPaPZWgL_xpVISYue6kPMZvM1oysVdBbmqiHxJfmrye2niK2xI1oBBWGbueXwE-rDeoCCo2LQiRwRa0Se0BBZ7Lg83A0pzhsWvSkvsplc4R4ZBza8pgwxKr5DVrrsim_XeIBhtunuxVDgIhY=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_67" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_70" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Projections</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">All maps are distorted to some degree. Your choice of coordinate system will affect the kind of distortion (shape, area, distance, direction) as well as the overall look of the map figure. Coordinate systems can be a complex topic but there are a few rules to keep in mind with journal map figures:</span></p><ul class="n8H08c UVNKR" style="list-style-type: square; margin-left: 0; margin-right: 0; padding: 0;"><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Never use a geographic coordinate system, always project your map data.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Avoid Mercator projections. They are severely distorted in area and shape in mid to upper latitudes. Knowledgeable readers may perceive its use as an indication that you are not familiar with best practice in geospatial analysis.&#160;&#160;</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Maps that depict densities (e.g. people per square mile) should always use an equal area projection so areas of different regions are comparable.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d eD0Rn" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Conformal projections which preserve the shape of map features are more familiar to most readers and therefore best used for general purpose maps or study area maps.</span></p></li></ul></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6dec52a75def9f7c_3" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SQ-nGUMmbwOFPMm6Oe8iXeUwGpn0boC3uCewcnJqAprPHtk8CUNH1pcBAk_yLd3DGpj3oJU6_OWQuh73IL-eErpwZ2ruMkbm-pRvw09YgQk5tvcSqcdDDyW3f_qtNPDkINrZp0iJvPS1PhmoMKa2a-4jQ7O-iHJNofSjvrLY-d96n9IJ7jySVS1F1ZqLG95cb4h2ZcPlJjvTPpFWiCDFjBsJYdvEL8tY8ZBA58=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_71" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_74" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Basemaps</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">QGIS and ArcGIS both have a number of built in 'basemap' services that allow you to quickly add a background map to your data. While these services speed up map creation and save you from having to build your own basemap, they are usually a poor choice for journal map figures.&#160; Basemap services are typically full color and fairly detailed. Their color and complexity will compete for attention with your data and flatten your maps visual hierarchy, making it harder for readers to zero in on your data and message.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">You can learn more about making your own basemaps in QGIS </span><a class="XqQF9c" href="https://youtu.be/lMfL9VsHcWA" target="_blank" style="color: inherit; text-decoration: none;"><span class="C9DxTc aw5Odc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700; text-decoration: underline;">here</span></a><span class="C9DxTc">.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.7b20ff9ea4c2b4e_5" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0STZcoocOYQA57xtKV1AE-udwHEasafX_WsIfY3rMkp9r5gPSMKhTyIf_UWxi74WdCwRDqifVvIL2_jtaKh_xQAuxQg9E_XHILuyWPAhSTQTIAXPdsS_vudDzBfJEp204v-Z8qpnKS2XCrAf526nZL7Olh8Oog0IebnCDCoah3P93gpu04y1h2PCyx4oJ4ckZK0jUvYAxQvSKy8=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img" style="width: 100.0000025129%; margin: 0% 0 0% -0.0000012564%"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_44" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc yYI8W HQwdzb"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_47" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><div id="h.ii21peoem6yn" class="GV3q8e yMxPgf aP9Z7e"></div><h3 id="h.ii21peoem6yn_l" dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q OmQG5e CDt4Ke"><div jscontroller="Ae65rd" jsaction="touchstart:UrsOsc; click:KjsqPd; focusout:QZoaZ; mouseover:y0pDld; mouseout:dq0hvd;fv1Rjc:jbFSOd;CrfLRd:SzACGe;" class="CjVfdc"><div class="PPhIP rviiZ" jsname="haAclf"><div role="presentation" class="U26fgb mUbCce fKz7Od LRAOtb Znu9nd M9Bg4d" jscontroller="mxS5xe" jsaction="click:cOuCgd; mousedown:UX7yZ; mouseup:lbsD7e; mouseenter:tfO1Yc; mouseleave:JywGue; focus:AHmuwe; blur:O22p3e; contextmenu:mg9Pef;" jsshadow="" aria-describedby="h.ii21peoem6yn_l" aria-label="Copy heading link" aria-disabled="false" data-tooltip="Copy heading link" aria-hidden="true" data-tooltip-position="top" data-tooltip-vertical-offset="12" data-tooltip-horizontal-offset="0"><a class="FKF6mc TpQm9d" href="#h.ii21peoem6yn" aria-label="Copy heading link" jsname="hiK3ld" role="button" aria-describedby="h.ii21peoem6yn_l"><div class="VTBa7b MbhUzd" jsname="ksKsZd"></div><span jsslot="" class="xjKiLb"><span class="Ce1Y1c" style="top: -11px"><svg class="OUGEr QdAdhf" width="22px" height="22px" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" /></span></span></a></div></div><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Layout</span></div></h3><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Layout covers both the various 'marginalia' elements that are added to a map in the composer, as well as their arrangement on the page.</span></p></div></div></div></div><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_126" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Titles</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Map figures rarely have proper titles. Instead you'll use the Figure Caption to title and briefly describe the map. See the captions section below for more detail.</span></p></div></div></div></div><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_131" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">North arrows and scale bars</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Maps of recognizable regions don't usually require north arrows or scale bars. North is assumed to be facing the top of page, so do include a directional indicator anytime you rotate a map.</span></p></div></div></div></div><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_135" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Legends</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Legends are only really needed when the symbols and features shown on your map are not recognizable on their own. A legend would not be needed for river lines or a study area box for instance. Whenever possible, rely on direct labeling instead of legends. For instance if you have a single point for a given data feature, don't add that symbol to a legend, label the feature directly in the map.</span></p></div></div></div></div><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_139" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Figure captions (replace)</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">One special thing about figures in journal articles is that they get captions! Captions are text that explains why you put this image in the article and what the reader needs to know about it. Caption text can sometimes take the place of a map legend. For example: </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-style: italic;">Figure 1: Study site locations. Black squares indicate sites treated with experimental weed killer and open circles indicate control sites.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Depending on the journal, the figure caption should contain the citation in the journal's preferred style for the data you used and the cartographer's name.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.6ef76e7bd495d32f_6" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OwsYgb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OwsYgb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6ef76e7bd495d32f_3" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OwsYgb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OwsYgb jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Be prepared to cite the data you used and the cartographer's name in the figure caption, as well as give any contextual information that the reader will need to know to interpret the map. (Myles, C., M.M. Tobias, &amp; I. McKinnon. 2021. “‘A big fish in a small pond’: How Arizona wine country was made” in </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-style: italic;">Agritourism, Wine Tourism, Craft Beer Tourism: Local Responses to peripherality through tourism niches.</span><span class="C9DxTc"> M. Giulia Pezzi, A. Faggian, N. Reid, eds. Routledge.)</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6ef76e7bd495d32f_0" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SSBYKSAtSPwWprskDjBhPTbrSEIlBkxERM0PKoAZm9lSe5Tzqy_aHm3SkfTmWvk6C5ISUf8UZVpj4tKBROC4CnAVL1QkdtMXcYArcs6K9CnS35EplLb0d_u4d8NsSzyNAokn-AgAAn_NonnaiUdx05iXX0eV5_kLPHHCwVqYmcH9OaVwdOnymmr3vZhrgzptE_5NAGGuAQVlTFmPEFNs6D8kWL7mPDpMxT6=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_141" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_144" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Alignment and frames</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Each of the various elements that can be added to a map in the layout or print composer can usually be placed anywhere on the page. Be careful to place these features where they won't obscure the main map or your data. Pay special attention to how each feature is spaced with relation to the map frame, page edge, or other features and try to use uniform spacing where possible to keep your map looking neat.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Avoid adding extra frames to these elements. Each new frame creates an obstacle to visual flow and can create distracting empty lines between elements (see the phenomena of </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">1+1=3</span><span class="C9DxTc">)</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_148" class="yaqOZd qeLZfd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_145" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SQom4eu77IlWh0oXQeMQW8VlRcyRLQsAYs-CQ-XfTGNwca7ucgE6OcMNdTpad0TCTITgfK13v7jGkpbLZ9482AssMTKLwu1pVtuy5138ZoTBNugiwz85CFe_By6eukSlfXHr4Gc3BDAiYMQO8ZmVBArJvVwtf9F9miN2bim9lz_oAR0fL1XqHyxMeu7m2lSQaFID_oqCo9iUq8=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img" style="width: 100.0000023842%;
