margin: -0.0000002172% 0 -0.0000002172% 0%"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_152" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><small dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TMjjoe CDt4Ke" style="display: block;"><span class="C9DxTc">The space between frames creates the illusion of a 3rd line (1+1=3). Notice how the removal of the frames declutters the image and lifts the figures in the visual hierarchy. </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-style: italic;">Envisioning Information,</span><span class="C9DxTc"> </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-style: italic;">Edward Tufte.</span></small></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_48" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_51" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><div id="h.2x22sqjlr7qr" class="GV3q8e yMxPgf aP9Z7e"></div><h3 id="h.2x22sqjlr7qr_l" dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q OmQG5e CDt4Ke"><div jscontroller="Ae65rd" jsaction="touchstart:UrsOsc; click:KjsqPd; focusout:QZoaZ; mouseover:y0pDld; mouseout:dq0hvd;fv1Rjc:jbFSOd;CrfLRd:SzACGe;" class="CjVfdc"><div class="PPhIP rviiZ" jsname="haAclf"><div role="presentation" class="U26fgb mUbCce fKz7Od LRAOtb Znu9nd M9Bg4d" jscontroller="mxS5xe" jsaction="click:cOuCgd; mousedown:UX7yZ; mouseup:lbsD7e; mouseenter:tfO1Yc; mouseleave:JywGue; focus:AHmuwe; blur:O22p3e; contextmenu:mg9Pef;" jsshadow="" aria-describedby="h.2x22sqjlr7qr_l" aria-label="Copy heading link" aria-disabled="false" data-tooltip="Copy heading link" aria-hidden="true" data-tooltip-position="top" data-tooltip-vertical-offset="12" data-tooltip-horizontal-offset="0"><a class="FKF6mc TpQm9d" href="#h.2x22sqjlr7qr" aria-label="Copy heading link" jsname="hiK3ld" role="button" aria-describedby="h.2x22sqjlr7qr_l"><div class="VTBa7b MbhUzd" jsname="ksKsZd"></div><span jsslot="" class="xjKiLb"><span class="Ce1Y1c" style="top: -11px"><svg class="OUGEr QdAdhf" width="22px" height="22px" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" /></span></span></a></div></div><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Exports</span></div></h3><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Refer to the publishers art specifications for export guidance and follow their recommendations. Most publishers will ask for a raster export (a .jpg. .tif. or .png). You may occasionally be asked for a vector based export like .ai or .eps. If you don't have access to Adobe Illustrator to create vector exports, you can substitute an .svg or .pdf file as both can be opened and edited in Illustrator.&#160;</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">If given the choice between vector and raster formats know that your vector map layout can be edited by the publisher but the raster format can not (not easily anyway). If you're a control freak like me this matters!</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_184" class="yaqOZd lQAHbd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_187" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><div id="h.3iex1262u59w" class="CobnVe yMxPgf aP9Z7e"></div><h2 id="h.3iex1262u59w_l" dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q JYVBee CDt4Ke"><div jscontroller="Ae65rd" jsaction="touchstart:UrsOsc; font-weight: 700;">Hands on exercise</span></div></h2><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Follow the steps below to create a sample map for journal publication, using the lessons from above.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_199" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.18f278c7f7fc607c_196" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><div id="h.kdlxrbh3f1tc" class="GV3q8e yMxPgf aP9Z7e"></div><h3 id="h.kdlxrbh3f1tc_l" dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q OmQG5e CDt4Ke"><div jscontroller="Ae65rd" jsaction="touchstart:UrsOsc; click:KjsqPd; focusout:QZoaZ; mouseover:y0pDld; mouseout:dq0hvd;fv1Rjc:jbFSOd;CrfLRd:SzACGe;" class="CjVfdc"><div class="PPhIP rviiZ" jsname="haAclf"><div role="presentation" class="U26fgb mUbCce fKz7Od LRAOtb Znu9nd M9Bg4d" jscontroller="mxS5xe" jsaction="click:cOuCgd; mousedown:UX7yZ; mouseup:lbsD7e; mouseenter:tfO1Yc; mouseleave:JywGue; focus:AHmuwe; blur:O22p3e; contextmenu:mg9Pef;" jsshadow="" aria-describedby="h.kdlxrbh3f1tc_l" aria-label="Copy heading link" aria-disabled="false" data-tooltip="Copy heading link" aria-hidden="true" data-tooltip-position="top" data-tooltip-vertical-offset="12" data-tooltip-horizontal-offset="0"><a class="FKF6mc TpQm9d" href="#h.kdlxrbh3f1tc" aria-label="Copy heading link" jsname="hiK3ld" role="button" aria-describedby="h.kdlxrbh3f1tc_l"><div class="VTBa7b MbhUzd" jsname="ksKsZd"></div><span jsslot="" class="xjKiLb"><span class="Ce1Y1c" style="top: -11px"><svg class="OUGEr QdAdhf" width="22px" height="22px" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" focusable="false" /></span></span></a></div></div><span class="C9DxTc">Premise</span></div></h3><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">On May 3rd, 2018, a new eruption began along the East Rift zone of Kīlauea volcano. The eruption forced the evacuation of 2 thousands residents, covered 13.7 square miles of land with fresh lava, and added 875 acres of new land to the island of Hawaii. It has been deemed the most destructive volcanic event in the US since the 1980 eruption of Mount St. Helens.&#160;</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">We'll be creating a map that overlays a portion of the 2018 main flow with several historic eruptions for a fictional journal article to be printed in the journal Nature.&#160;</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf class="C9DxTc">If you haven't already, download the data folder </span><a class="XqQF9c" href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1O59PW_6YVcrNjhbQ-7Jr9u-w3rMpOKU6/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank" style="color: inherit; text-decoration: none;"><span class="C9DxTc aw5Odc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700; text-decoration: underline;">here</span></a><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;"> </span><span class="C9DxTc">and extract it to your desktop.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Our project data is organized into the subfolders listed below:</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">HI_base_data</span><span class="C9DxTc">: contains Hawaii state outlines in two different scales and a buffer layer for adding a water effect.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">contact_polygons</span><span class="C9DxTc">: a series of polygon layers for the lava flow contact surfaces of three historic eruptions in the Kilauea East Rift area (1790, 1840, 1955), as well as the line layer for the main fissures.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">KIL_2018_raster</span><span class="C9DxTc">: a composite raster of the 2018 East Rift eruption combining the thermal IR band (band 10) from a series of Landsat 8 images captured between 5/16/2018 and 6/17/2018.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">KIL_2018_poly:</span><span class="C9DxTc"> a single vector (polygon) feature representing the 2018 East Rift eruption.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb JNdkSc SQVYQc baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">A note about using the Layer Styling menu: You get different options for setting the symbols style, or picking colors, based on which button you select in the menu. The standard Fill menu only lets you change the fill color while the Simple Fill menu lets you control everything from fill and stroke colors, to line stroke thickness, to corner styles and a bunch of other symbol soptions.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Likewise, the buttons for picking colors have different menus as well. The small drop down arrow next to the color swatch brings up the basic color picker, while clicking on the color swatch button itself brings up a more advanced color menu with sliders for HSV and RGB values, as well as a transparency slider.</span></p><br></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">1.5 </span><span class="C9DxTc">mm.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" class="C9DxTc">.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Singleband pseudocolor.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Because rasters represent continuous data (unlike discrete vector data), our new layer covers up the other layers underneath it. Setting the layer to be partially transparent doesn't work as it would tint everything in the map orange. Luckily we can adjust the Min and Max symbology values to narrow the range of pixels we are symbolizing, making the rest of the raster disappear. We'll use a range of values that just covers the main lava flow, or regions of highest accumulation in the lava flow composite.</span></p><ol class="n8H08c BKnRcf" style="list-style-type: decimal; margin-left: 0; margin-right: 0; padding: 0;"><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Set the Min value to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">130000</span><span class="C9DxTc"> and the Max value to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">205092</span><span class="C9DxTc">.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Under the Classify button, check the box for </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Clip out of range values</span><span class="C9DxTc">.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Click </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Apply</span><span class="C9DxTc"> to see your changes.</span></p></li></ol></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Point Symbols and Labels</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Before we move on to laying out or map for exporting, lets add a little context to the location. We can't map as much detail for these small maps as we would for a full size map, but adding a few small location points with names can help orient the viewer.</span></p><ol class="n8H08c BKnRcf" style="list-style-type: decimal; margin-left: 0; margin-right: 0; padding: 0;"><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">In the Layers window, turn on the Pop_Places layer.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">In the Layer Styling menu in the symbol preview window under Marker, select the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">dot black</span><span class="C9DxTc"> symbol.</span></p></li></ol><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">If you would like to modify the styling of this symbol select the Simple Marker menu and use the new menu to change your symbols shape, color, size, and outline.</span></p><ol class="n8H08c BKnRcf" style="list-style-type: decimal; margin-left: 0; margin-right: 0; padding: 0;"><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">In the Layer Styling menu, select the Labels option.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Change the drop down from </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">No Labels</span><span class="C9DxTc"> to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Single Labels</span><span class="C9DxTc">.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Set the Value drop down to the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">name </span><span class="C9DxTc">field.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">On the Text sub menu, adjust you label size to suit your map. Try 14 points</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Change the Style to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Bold</span><span class="C9DxTc">.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Finally, to make sure your labels are not covering up their points, go to the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Placement</span><span class="C9DxTc"> sub menu, select </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Cartographic</span><span class="C9DxTc"> placement and set the offset value to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">4 mm</span><span class="C9DxTc">.</span></p></li></ol><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-right: 0.0pt; text-align: left;"><span class="C9DxTc">This should get you most of the way there. You play around the rest of the settings to modify placement as needed, or add halos or masks to the labels to help them stand out against your map background.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf JNdkSc SQVYQc yYI8W HQwdzb"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.1c4dc10545d7a03f_16" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SSbr0DlS0R6LjjtJe0BUDvpiq_2Ux93LlA_rDBobW75BtDxkTLsRwZruxxZkBab_V0XiAUcPs7hr_IymeyLNzdyCQZaLWTrVCDVEcDIGlOp7NsMghz6-eZSv_M02az3CT0rRbbgm33KgLa318TAxM2VDQy-M23N7JY1R0P3o18x1qFlT_M0G_60Pzmz6q6KSmEitod7mp1l7OCOBA20a_1OLXkQpNqncz7v=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img" style="width: 100.000002053%; sidebar.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="background-color: transparent; border-bottom: none; border-left: none; border-right: none; border-top: none; line-height: 1.38; margin-bottom: 6.0pt; margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; margin-top: 0.0pt; padding-bottom: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; padding-right: 0.0pt; padding-top: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #000000; font-family: Arial; font-size: 11.0pt; font-style: normal; font-weight: 400; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline;">Click in the empty print layout page to drag a box for your map (or simply click in the layout and enter a specific map layout dimension).</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="background-color: transparent; border-bottom: none; border-left: none; border-right: none; border-top: none; line-height: 1.38; margin-bottom: 6.0pt; margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; margin-top: 0.0pt; padding-bottom: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; padding-right: 0.0pt; padding-top: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #000000; font-family: Arial; font-weight: 400;">You can refine the maps placement with the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #000000; font-family: Arial; font-size: 11.0pt; font-style: normal; font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline;">Select/Move Item</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #000000; font-family: Arial; font-weight: 700;"> </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #000000; font-family: Arial; font-weight: 400;">tool, or go to the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #000000; font-family: Arial; font-weight: 700;">Position and Size</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #000000; font-family: Arial; font-weight: 400;"> options in the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #000000; font-family: Arial; font-weight: 700;">Items Properties</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #000000; font-family: Arial; font-weight: 400;"> menu. Use either method to snap the embedded map to the page edges.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.1c4dc10545d7a03f_0" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.3928d1f10baae8d4_42" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="background-color: transparent; border-bottom: none; border-left: none; border-right: none; border-top: none; line-height: 1.38; margin-bottom: 12.0pt; margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; margin-top: 0.0pt; padding-bottom: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; padding-right: 0.0pt; padding-top: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 400; vertical-align: baseline;">Use the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 700; vertical-align: baseline;">Move Item Content</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 400; vertical-align: baseline;"> tool to interactively pan and zoom the data around inside the map area. You can play around with this until you get a fit that looks good. Or you can set a specific scale in the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 700; vertical-align: baseline;">Item Properties</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 400; vertical-align: baseline;"> menu. Just find the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 700; vertical-align: baseline;">Main Properties</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 400; vertical-align: baseline;"> item and in the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 700; vertical-align: baseline;">Scale</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 400; vertical-align: baseline;"> dialogue enter your desired map scale. I've set mine to 70000 (or 1:70,000).</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="background-color: transparent; border-bottom: none; border-left: none; border-right: none; border-top: none; line-height: 1.38; margin-bottom: 12.0pt; margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; margin-top: 0.0pt; padding-bottom: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; padding-right: 0.0pt; padding-top: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 400; vertical-align: baseline;">Make adjustments until you're happy with how the map looks. Ideally we'll have the relevant map data centered filling our map layout.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-R6PoUb purZT-AhqUyc-R6PoUb class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc yYI8W HQwdzb"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_2" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Setting a map reference scale</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Occasionally due to the interactions between map layer symbology and the layout page size and map scale, symbols and labels that appear correct in the main GIS view will look way too small or large in the print composer. The solution is to set a </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Fixed Reference Scale</span><span class="C9DxTc"> on the individual map layers.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Go back to the main map view in QGIS and open the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Properties</span><span class="C9DxTc"> for </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Pop_Places</span><span class="C9DxTc">. Under the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Rendering</span><span class="C9DxTc"> property menu check the box for </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Fixed Reference Scale </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;">and then use the drop down at the right to select the scal</span><span class="C9DxTc">e from your Print Composer window. Go back to the Print Composer view and hit the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Update Map Preview</span><span class="C9DxTc"> button to refresh the view.</span></p></div></div></div></div><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype"><div id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_19" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-EehZO Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-EehZO jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SQuBELcbytPNpcKld1em-eB2kxQX1YH-sQX63e_gS6Qfe_rxmjWSAr7Qd3oPTNBS7PGqvLR5B7LxR5VFA6_ghQDIbANTtaDtvSajg9AG1ueThH4XcvlZBSzVJUtjtHYxwpzjVfvaWkKUgeLw-KqC29zh7ob8RuW_Pi-aVaGpWZeY-yvbq-ZOOp6667W3pIH5rIV6hP2LqOy-sZcUWn5PrUw8WLd2ERu7rMmU2I=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_35" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_32" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Add floating text</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Any labels that can't be created directly from your map features, can be manually placed in the layout view. Select the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Add Label</span><span class="C9DxTc"> tool from the print composer toolbar. Place the crosshair above the main lava region and then click to drag a small text box. A new "</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">lorem ipsum</span><span class="C9DxTc">" entry will appear in the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Items</span><span class="C9DxTc"> list. Under the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Main Properties</span><span class="C9DxTc"> dialogue replace the default text with </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">East Rift Zone</span><span class="C9DxTc">.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">In the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Appearance</span><span class="C9DxTc"> property menu click on the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Font</span><span class="C9DxTc"> dropdown to change the font from regular style and size to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Bold 16 points</span><span class="C9DxTc">.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Use the Select/Move tool to adjust the text ppacement.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_48" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SRyXCkGoCcVocLupogp2R3-fLJgY9sryJHDjob2h5MNXCaYIRPXm90gB59ZEkvhwxFFjnHRwc4q7nZQ2AFiUYKh5XEZYLCbbKqhBImB0d1FU7S8c_fre6T08CdX-HkpoFXy1UCZffWohY7gC741ldcVd9r8Itt39CU4lz1-9gML48yDrpSb7MFVLIM2HLayNs0heOMTKETNGO0DN2oScAcGaaK9JoFxLocK=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_31" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_28" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Add a legend</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Select the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Add Legend</span><span class="C9DxTc"> tool from the print composer toolbar. Click to drag a small box in the upper rigt hand corner of your layout. The new legend box will initially show all layers in the main map, even if they are not currently set to be visible. We can remove these from the Item Properties menu under </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Legend Items</span><span class="C9DxTc">. Go to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Legend Items</span><span class="C9DxTc"> and uncheck </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Auto Update</span><span class="C9DxTc">. Then select any layers that are not the lava composite raster or the the three contact polygons. Use the red minus symbol below the Legend Items list to remove those entries from the legend.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">If you double click a layer name in the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Legend Items</span><span class="C9DxTc"> box you can update the layer alias. Change "</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">lava composite b10</span><span class="C9DxTc">" to "</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">New Flow</span><span class="C9DxTc">". Change "</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">1955_contacts</span><span class="C9DxTc">" to "</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">1955 Contacts</span><span class="C9DxTc">" (repeat for the remaining contacts layers).</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Scroll down to the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Symbol</span><span class="C9DxTc"> property. Uncheck the box next to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Draw stroke for raster symbols</span><span class="C9DxTc">.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">If your legend has a frame you can remove it by unchecking the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Frame</span><span class="C9DxTc"> property. Or if you want to keep the frame but modify the symbology, toggle the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Frame</span><span class="C9DxTc"> property open and make your changes.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">It's important that items like the legend be evenly spaced off the map margins whenever possible. using the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Select/Move</span><span class="C9DxTc"> tool select the legend and drag it into the far right corner until it snaps up against the layout edge. Now using the arrow keys on your keyboard move the legend down and to the left an even number of steps, try 5 down and 5 left.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_51" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SSAmYGKvEaBEunPy0yJi6Gblb2HDeFaSmr70p3Hc-BSYaQBFALrsNOaF5qZTInYhaNfdPPrGbX4_YjMMLljstC1u36_IoEWTAvaub-4-aSzttgfPsu-3DB13H9-8aU4SDWWcGLRTzCQbUlyPGjC0yf1IuTvMLJVt3Lpn8iYLTO2UqkChfYjTn4CrWa3ACohIR_Bk8eyG0zYzISNhvFR6g0V3g6jj_O6rfYU_Fs=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_44" class="yaqOZd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_47" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-OiUrBf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Add a </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">scale bar</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;">Select the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Add </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Scale Bar</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;"> tool from the print composer toolbar. C</span><span class="C9DxTc">l</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;">ick and drag a small box in the lower right corner of your</span><span class="C9DxTc"> layout.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">In the Item Properties menu under Main Properties set </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Style</span><span class="C9DxTc"> to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Line Ticks Up</span><span class="C9DxTc">.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Under the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Units </span><span class="C9DxTc">property</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;"> </span><span class="C9DxTc">you can set the scale bar to Feet, Meters, Miles, Kilometers etc. Leave yours set to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Kilometers</span><span class="C9DxTc">.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Under </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Segments</span><span class="C9DxTc"> you can adjust the number of ticks or the unit intervals as needed. Leave your set to Fixed width 1 unit with 2 right segments.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_54" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ktthjf-AhqUyc-II5mzb jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SSYbU351UUQHuCrVWRhGXrlyJxrfxjtveFHmn_AM-VWgrvQCQvg0ikI49v0zMPmekj9aCdBWGv879gE2HT9-j1Secwg363q3I5LYXeO4yG7tBXUfU19dtyFOnudBRjqpkMdhre4oap01H2atvJ582DWGEjLbCyv8InrPMyOLQLnBeweQb9kiix62Jbk9TayOvtjaetavOUD_SzAUuOYOipgEUL0fmCiX_gc=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_39" class="yaqOZd qeLZfd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-Clt0zb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-Clt0zb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_36" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-Clt0zb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-Clt0zb jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Add a locator map with extent indicator</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Activate the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Map 1</span><span class="C9DxTc"> item in your layout view.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Under the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Layers</span><span class="C9DxTc"> item property, check the box for </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Lock Layers</span><span class="C9DxTc"> then check the box for </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Lock styles for layers</span><span class="C9DxTc"> (this freezes the current layout so changes to the main map will not update in the layout view).</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Return to the main map view in QGIS. Right click on the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">coast_UTM5</span><span class="C9DxTc"> layer and select </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Duplicate</span><span class="C9DxTc">.&#160; Uncheck all layers besides </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">coast_UTM5 copy</span><span class="C9DxTc">. Change the new layer style to full back with no outline.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf JNdkSc SQVYQc yYI8W HQwdzb"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_57" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0STZV9FKF0IPfYS5lSLhwZ7yUwEp2mTWLUH-bBBZ6MyQ900fAnNPXe8Ff34pFH0Ppzy3vM9w5n4gFYBq6am-G_ICDZ1gVSfuGWVMxMyHoA6sNbgjOyDjr4wHJKpFBfGViRNcqxQtLR-N0BTq6MpOzMdce6rNtxv9RhMzYGEWGmDsLUYNaqCmCelB81QpYMl6IsVuTwjTe7SbHyiY9UwV1CEnqyBkNa_T0DcTZ8g=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype"><div id="h.6539d0555f9ce5ed_60" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec wHaque g5GTcb QaN0Zc QjXeod"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SRjUH7rmT3L4_8HkBfvq63MqHAHHTc7KvloXdFSRTozeRjiw0SMF2SuNEcRFCjEdsE23kfuDscpSd1oTxMqUHBr3PJgaxJKwkBFH8vsd6K8FEobuhjYBda8lRMIMNZzXDqGZ6RSQsha4ANd0-L6uqBa20qV5zpPwxo8g0TRnM1zDK6uVfJ9s1UvSoizs3gq7HdK-eHhE7ypnMsCuFw3ch9WA2sng4-hdNEVGFs=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img" style="width: 100.0000020797%; margin: 0% 0 0% -0.0000010398%"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.6dec52a75def9f7c_9" class="yaqOZd qeLZfd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-Clt0zb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-Clt0zb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6dec52a75def9f7c_12" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-Clt0zb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-Clt0zb jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;">Go back into the print composer and use the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Add Map</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;"> tool to drag a new map frame in the upper left corner of the layout. Adjust the frame so that it does not cover any map labels.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;">In the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Item Properties</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;"> menu, find the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Background</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;"> property for the new map frame. Click the color swatch button to open the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Select Background Color</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;"> dialog box. Find the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Opacity</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;"> slider and set it to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">50%</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;">.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;">Toggle open the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Overviews</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;"> property. Hit the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700; vertical-align: baseline;">+</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;"> to add a new overview and set the Map frame to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Map1</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;">. Set the frame style to red fill.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf JNdkSc SQVYQc yYI8W HQwdzb"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.6dec52a75def9f7c_19" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SSWSWJ3rbp7N-euLTLR7oUwAcU2R1kd7heq9BRzUBuRGhExgybJwX40009I3YYkuZ5wpSgO43MA2acQefPbdyt5KbUP4J8VJSGqrmBn8FQEJLiSexQJCNcEyoa0HPji4YLv2U5TtbzHfes3yUzaUhEbpQOsppwuNyK2ay25MvFt55IHXVBLm_-0U5l9VCwQSkpJlYgke0Dr24B_yQhe1FSG6g1a91eQpLNcLHM=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img" style="width: 100.0000030803%; font-weight: 700;">Aligning map items</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Under the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">View</span><span class="C9DxTc"> menu you'll see some options for </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Show Grid</span><span class="C9DxTc"> and </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Snap to Grid </span><span class="C9DxTc">as well as </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Snap to Guides</span><span class="C9DxTc"> and </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Smart Guides</span><span class="C9DxTc">. You can use these settings to help you move and align items around the map layout. Try it now with the Legend box. If you don't like the default snapping go ahead and turn everything off.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Be sure to pay attention to how different items on the layout are aligned to each other the map edges. In general you want to keep even spacing between items and between the edges of the map frame.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.1bfdb522b9ece78_9" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SSKsaSxRcQJjVBuwTxDgHxQnxmsUVuzS6X4E0DkKrG63wRKXOPBS3HN11bs0sm4QY_07rimYLK4U6Bass3-1u3opxKdvso2zFKNtuzS6qZp2wRDEB1j4JmfHXKUltWEJZvPihE10Z4GB6htRkotbi1vB3dMqNPpwTOYSL46Hk-TU5usydrXmh24jFZw0rZTJH8nCnaM11TlvQCuI0Jl2bngQPEftkKQPNus=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img" style="width: 100%; font-weight: 700;">Add an image background or overlay</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">You can add a custom image as a background filler or to overlay the main map for special effects like a paper texture.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Use the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Add Picture</span><span class="C9DxTc"> tool to drag a box around the map layout. In </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Item</span><span class="C9DxTc"> </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Properties</span><span class="C9DxTc"> for the new picture layer change the file type to Raster Image and then browse to the lab data files and select </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">grunge_paper.jpg</span><span class="C9DxTc"> from the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">images</span><span class="C9DxTc"> folder.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">Under the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Rendering</span><span class="C9DxTc"> menu set the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Blending mode</span><span class="C9DxTc"> to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Multiply</span><span class="C9DxTc"> and pull the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">opacity</span><span class="C9DxTc"> back to around </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">80%</span><span class="C9DxTc">.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf JNdkSc SQVYQc yYI8W HQwdzb"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.1bfdb522b9ece78_19" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SRo8ZJ6KA8VEeVXSGIRyEaXAELwj7523m9gGw_4zrD5bu5QqX84MnE9j6ION_WKqKeUTbWZbcwzzTtIMlEhroH61h6ITkfrdENM_2e2RDv7TtEmubibL1ZU0CBxAYGmxK50o1dFs_chfz0RF-Xpj_bJTzok8vGefQI3Zk9LA8LNMy6uPCp6Qjhs0cgMqdwq4ihgp8aNdbGxU0XnZetfZLSYfc2xINvUqBNB=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img" style="width: 100.0000003335%; padding-left: 0.0pt; padding-right: 0.0pt; padding-top: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 700; vertical-align: baseline;">Export Map</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="background-color: transparent; border-bottom: none; border-left: none; border-right: none; border-top: none; line-height: 1.38; margin-bottom: 12.0pt; margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; margin-top: 0.0pt; padding-bottom: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; padding-right: 0.0pt; padding-top: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 400; vertical-align: baseline;">Most publishers require an image export of your final map. A .jpg is the most common file type but you may also be asked for a .tiff or a .png.</span></p><ol class="n8H08c BKnRcf" style="list-style-type: decimal; margin-left: 0; margin-right: 0; padding: 0;"><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="background-color: transparent; padding-left: 0.0pt; padding-right: 0.0pt; padding-top: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 400; vertical-align: baseline;">In the Print Layout window, select the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 700; vertical-align: baseline;">Export as Image</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 400; vertical-align: baseline;"> icon.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="background-color: transparent; border-bottom: none; border-left: none; border-right: none; border-top: none; line-height: 1.38; margin-bottom: 12.0pt; margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; margin-top: 0.0pt; padding-bottom: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; padding-right: 0.0pt; padding-top: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 400; vertical-align: baseline;">Select the export folder, give your map export a new name and click </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 700; vertical-align: baseline;">Save</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 400; vertical-align: baseline;">.</span></p></li></ol><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="background-color: transparent; border-bottom: none; border-left: none; border-right: none; border-top: none; line-height: 1.38; margin-bottom: 12.0pt; margin-left: 0.0pt; margin-right: 0.0pt; margin-top: 0.0pt; padding-bottom: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; padding-right: 0.0pt; padding-top: 0.0pt; text-align: left; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 400; vertical-align: baseline;">A new window will open where you can set the</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 700; vertical-align: baseline;"> Image Export Options</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="color: #24292e; font-family: Arial; font-size: 12.0pt; font-weight: 400; vertical-align: baseline;">. Refer to your publishers specifications for things like DPI and color space. In our case we'll use 300 dpi. The output will be an RGB color file, your publisher can convert this to CMYK if needed or you can resave the file yourself in something like Photoshop or GIMP.</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-ibL1re Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-ibL1re JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0STvGRnE1lneCm81FsAAard_tptdifl_AeJyonLKrTK1O0FIVChCr7Mv1hRkJ_lTc5rKBiC_Epq10jyqsaKusujza8OXnTuprqmCUcqJsQD2-Why-Zzr0w1bvEs6HYmv0l7wMGVM47nAp4hXJCfbVws0vXk5zc8hIovUG4RZj108leZ7-VOBoTxbc6A79pzG52VuX9YOCUPqtO7GELMucAARrxgGbopcXdZe7X4=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-II5mzb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-II5mzb JNdkSc SQVYQc L6cTce-purZT L6cTce-pSzOP"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="h.2a95d786ff829417_0" class="yaqOZd qeLZfd"><div class="IFuOkc"></div><div class="mYVXT"><div class="LS81yb VICjCf j5pSsc db35Fc" tabindex="-1"><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-Clt0zb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-Clt0zb purZT-AhqUyc-II5mzb ZcASvf-AhqUyc-II5mzb pSzOP-AhqUyc-qWD73c Ktthjf-AhqUyc-qWD73c JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.2a95d786ff829417_3" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-Clt0zb Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-Clt0zb jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd mGzaTb Depvyb baZpAe"><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Extra: Convert layout to a Small Multiple</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">A </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Small Multiple</span><span class="C9DxTc"> is a data visualization method that places a simplified version of the map into a grid or map series where the base map remains exactly the same and only the overlaying data changes from one frame to the other. It is useful for showing data that changes over time or comparing different layers of data for the same geography.</span></p><p dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke"><span class="C9DxTc">If you want to be absolutely sure you are using the same background map scale and extent set up a </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Spatial Bookmark</span><span class="C9DxTc"> before you start making the separate maps.</span></p><ol class="n8H08c BKnRcf" style="list-style-type: decimal; margin-left: 0; margin-right: 0; padding: 0;"><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">From the Print Composer, save your current layout and then click the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">New Layout</span><span class="C9DxTc"> icon to start a new map.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Set the new map page dimensions to </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">4" x 16".</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Add a new Map frame and set it to be </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">3.5" x 3.5"</span><span class="C9DxTc"> and position it on the left side of the page with a small even margin to the left top and bottom.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">In the main QGIS app, turn off all layers except the island background and the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">1790 contact</span><span class="C9DxTc"> polygon layer.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">In the Print Composer, hit the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Refresh view</span><span class="C9DxTc"> icon so the print map matches the GIS view.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Once the map looks good, go into the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Item Properties</span><span class="C9DxTc"> and check the boxes for</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;"> Lock layers</span><span class="C9DxTc"> and </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Lock styles for layers.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Now copy and paste the current map frame to make a duplicate map. Align the new map to the right of the first map frame.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Go into the Item Properties and uncheck </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Lock layers</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;"> and </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Lock styles for layers</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Go back into QGIS and add the 1840 contact layer so both 1790 and 1840 now show in the map.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Back in the Print Composer, refresh the 2nd map frame to show your changes then re lock </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Lock layers</span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-variant: normal;"> and </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-variant: normal; font-weight: 700;">Lock styles for layers.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Repeat this process two more times, adding the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">1955 contact</span><span class="C9DxTc"> to the 3rd map frame, then finally adding the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">2018 eruption</span><span class="C9DxTc"> raster t the 4th map frame.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">Use the </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Align</span><span class="C9DxTc"> and </span><span class="C9DxTc" style="font-family: Lato, Arial; font-weight: 700;">Distribute</span><span class="C9DxTc"> tools to line all of the map frames up in the same center and add the same amount of spacing between each frame.</span></p></li><li dir="ltr" class="zfr3Q TYR86d lsiHE" style="margin-left: 15.0pt;"><p dir="ltr" role="presentation" class="zfr3Q CDt4Ke" style="margin-left: 0.0pt; padding-left: 0.0pt; text-indent: 0.0pt;"><span class="C9DxTc">With the full set of map frames selcted, use your arrow keys to nudge them around for better spacing in the page.</span></p></li></ol></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf JNdkSc SQVYQc"><div class="JNdkSc-SmKAyb LkDMRd"><div jscontroller="sGwD4d" jsaction="zXBUYb:zTPCnb;zQF9Uc:Qxe3nd;" jsname="F57UId"><div class="oKdM2c ZZyype Kzv0Me"><div id="h.1042c07409bc40c6_0" class="hJDwNd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ft7HRd-AhqUyc-c5RTEf purZT-AhqUyc-c5RTEf ZcASvf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf pSzOP-AhqUyc-c5RTEf Ktthjf-AhqUyc-c5RTEf jXK9ad D2fZ2 zu5uec OjCsFc dmUFtb wHaque g5GTcb"><div class="jXK9ad-SmKAyb"><div class="tyJCtd baZpAe"><div class="t3iYD"><img src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/sitesv/APaQ0SR0LGaEkMXaXTHcYiHhu3MdfT7j_se5Bo1zII5le2mnTm3oTp4W4TOkSAyXqHlPzMn58me8KYmbNPbCZXYnUndPX_CS7C6lChmAYPLk2mZ-Y6B6WcaRdcsXudT0dZy9a2ZtiEepayvTnTkXCpI_PCa3RiTVdkxlJwptshzjJqR3yuVml5iz-8rQQysJbvvYNg1_S6QKeaoYDUzV2gGE2HilLFNYKgx3ViMv0Ds=w1280" class="CENy8b" role="img" style="width: 100%;
